- **builder**: Executes a plan in one context, uninterrupted, with progress reports and an optional end-of-run review. ([ubiqel/dotfiles](https://github.com/ubiqel/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/common/home/.config/opencode/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Sonnet-powered implementation agent. Use during implementation to execute exactly one step from an approved plan's TODO checklist - given the step text, its verify condition, and the files it may touch. Independent steps run as multiple builders in parallel. Implements test-first, runs the step's verification, reports changes and results. Never commits. ([UD-UD/staff-engineer-plugin](https://github.com/UD-UD/staff-engineer-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Executes the implementation plan, manages tasks, and writes the code. ([Udaradg/ai-coding-assistants](https://github.com/Udaradg/ai-coding-assistants/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/builder.agent.md))
- **builder**: Implements one plan row that has a named acceptance gate. Use for feature work and refactors; never dispatch without naming the gate that proves it. ([udsy19/Qollab-Hackathon](https://github.com/udsy19/Qollab-Hackathon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/builder.md) · 3 copies)
- **builder**: Implements one task in its own worktree, test first: engine (Rust) first, then the web glue. Commits on bunny/&lt;task&gt;; never pushes. ([Univers42/hydrawn](https://github.com/Univers42/hydrawn/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: High-throughput Go developer executing approved specs ([urban233/ntn-ingest](https://github.com/urban233/ntn-ingest/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/builder.md) · 2 copies)
- **builder**: Implements exactly ONE queued saas-factory task (TASK-#####) end to end — code plus tests — inside its declared file scope, reusing the registry instead of duplicating, then hands off for verification. Use for any implementation work from the queue. Works as a subagent or agent-team teammate. ([usmanali4073/claude-code-saas-factory-plugin](https://github.com/usmanali4073/claude-code-saas-factory-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Reaches exactly one goal end to end and proves it. Use for any change that touches code. Give it one goal, its files, and the test that proves it. ([vcentai/claude-code-systems](https://github.com/vcentai/claude-code-systems/blob/HEAD/agent-fleet/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Builds one Vextrus ticket to its committed acceptance tests, in a cloud session (pushes only its own branch) or a local worktree (commits only), and finishes with the Factory-State trailer the factory reads. Its contract is this file; a cloud launch prompt begins "Follow .claude/agents/builder.md". Use for every ticket's build after its acceptance-writer has committed. ([vextrus/vextrus-cubit](https://github.com/vextrus/vextrus-cubit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: RBB builder. Builds a change from its approved spec alone, verifies each example before and after, and logs every decision and deviation from the plan. Started only by RBB workflows. ([vholley/rbb](https://github.com/vholley/rbb/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Use when the task fits an existing pattern in the codebase, touches a bounded set of files, and the open choices are local (names, small structure, edge cases). Do not use when no pattern exists to follow, the change is cross-cutting, or a wrong call is expensive, such as data loss, auth, concurrency, migrations, or public API (use specialist instead). ([vicainelli/rungs](https://github.com/vicainelli/rungs/blob/HEAD/plugins/agents/copilot/agents/builder.agent.md) · 3 copies)
- **builder**: Implements one PR end to end from a written brief, in its own git worktree. Use for feature work, refactors and multi-file changes that need the session's model. Give it the brief, the worktree path and the verify gate. ([vieko/.dotfiles](https://github.com/vieko/.dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/.claude/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Well-specified implementation work that spans several files, where the brief says what to build and how to check it. Use it for features, fixes and migrations when the design is already settled. ([viktoravelino/agents-config](https://github.com/viktoravelino/agents-config/blob/HEAD/shared/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Use to implement code against a design that is already settled — writing and editing source, tests, migrations, and config. This is the default agent for anything that produces a diff. Give it the decision, not the problem. If a real design question surfaces mid-task it stops and escalates rather than deciding for itself. ([vimukthiD/claude-plugins](https://github.com/vimukthiD/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev-kit/agents/builder.md))

- **builder**: You are builder, a senior software engineer on this team, working in the repository checkout in your directory. ([vivekyarra/dibs](https://github.com/vivekyarra/dibs/blob/HEAD/agents/builder.md))
